+"""
+Flight Monitor — Database Layer (SQLite thread-safe)
+基於既有微技能: sqlite3_thread_safe
+"""
+import sqlite3
+import threading
+from contextlib import contextmanager
+from pathlib import Path
+from datetime import datetime
+from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
+
+
+SCHEMA = """
+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS flights (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+    flight_no TEXT NOT NULL,
+    airline TEXT NOT NULL,
+    origin TEXT NOT NULL,
+    destination TEXT NOT NULL,
+    departure_date TEXT NOT NULL,
+    departure_time TEXT,
+    arrival_time TEXT,
+    duration_min INTEGER,
+    stops INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
+    aircraft TEXT,
+    UNIQUE(flight_no, departure_date)
+);
+
+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS price_history (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+    flight_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+    price REAL NOT NULL,
+    currency TEXT DEFAULT 'TWD',
+    cabin_class TEXT DEFAULT 'economy',
+    seats_left INTEGER,
+    recorded_at TEXT NOT NULL,
+    FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights(id),
+    UNIQUE(flight_id, recorded_at, cabin_class)
+);
+
+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_price_flight ON price_history(flight_id);
+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_price_recorded ON price_history(recorded_at);
+
+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS price_alerts (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+    flight_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+    threshold_price REAL NOT NULL,
+    direction TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(direction IN ('below', 'above')),
+    triggered_at TEXT,
+    FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights(id)
+);
+
+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scrape_log (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+    source TEXT NOT NULL,
+    query_json TEXT,
+    started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
+    finished_at TEXT,
+    flights_fetched INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
+    status TEXT DEFAULT 'running',
+    error_msg TEXT
+);
+
+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scrape_status ON scrape_log(status);
+"""
+
+
+class FlightDB:
+    """Thread-safe SQLite wrapper for flight data persistence.
+    Reuses the `sqlite3_thread_safe` micro-skill pattern.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, db_path: str):
+        self.db_path = db_path
+        self._lock = threading.RLock()
+        Path(db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, check_same_thread=False)
+        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

+"""
+Flight Scraper — fetches flight data with retry/backoff.
+
+Per architect_feedback.md §2:
+> 爬蟲與資料抓取模組必須設計得夠強健,具備重試機制 (Retry/Backoff)
+
+We support TWO modes:
+1. LIVE mode — actually hits a public flight API endpoint (AviationStack-style).
+2. SIMULATE mode — generates realistic synthetic flight data with stochastic
+   price walks so the system can be demonstrated end-to-end without network.
+
+The mode is selected by env var FLIGHT_MODE (default "simulate").
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import time
+import random
+import logging
+import hashlib
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Callable
+
+import requests
+
+from .database import FlightDB
+
+logger = logging.getLogger("flight.scraper")
+
+
+# ---------- Retry / Backoff ----------
+
+class RetryableHTTPError(Exception):
+    """Raised for transient HTTP errors that warrant retry."""
+    def __init__(self, status: int, url: str):
+        super().__init__(f"HTTP {status} from {url}")
+        self.status = status
+
+
+def fetch_with_retry(
+    url: str,
+    params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
+    headers: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
+    timeout: float = 10.0,
+    max_retries: int = 3,
+    backoff_base: float = 1.5,
+) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Fetch URL with exponential backoff retry.
+    Per `python-resilience` pattern: 3 retries, exponential backoff,
+    separate handling for 4xx (don't retry) vs 5xx / network (retry).
+    """
+    headers = headers or {}
+    last_err: Optional[Exception] = None
+    for attempt in range(1, max_retries + 1):
+        try:
+            resp = requests.get(url, params=params, headers=headers,
+                                timeout=timeout)
+            if resp.status_code == 200:
+                return resp.json()
+            if 400 <= resp.status_code < 500 and resp.status_code != 429:
+                # Client error — not retryable, but DO surface to caller
+                raise RetryableHTTPError(resp.status_code, url)
+            # 5xx or 429 — retryable
+            last_err = RetryableHTTPError(resp.status_code, url)
+            logger.warning(
+                "fetch_with_retry attempt %d/%d got HTTP %d, will retry",
+                attempt, max_retries, resp.status_code,
+            )
+        except requests.RequestException as e:
+            last_err = e
+            logger.warning("fetch_with_retry attempt %d/%d got %s",
+                           attempt, max_retries, type(e).__name__)
+
+        if attempt < max_retries:
+            sleep_s = (backoff_base ** attempt) + random.uniform(0, 0.5)
+            time.sleep(sleep_s)
+
+    raise last_err or RuntimeError("fetch_with_retry: exhausted retries")

+"""
+Price Predictor — trend + volatility analysis for flight prices.
+
+Per architect_feedback §2 (Async Architecture & Data Flow):
+> 所有後台排程或事件處理需全面採用 asyncio 提升吞吐量
+
+The predictor is pure-Python (deterministic), so it's safe to call
+from any context. The async wrapper in monitor.py calls it from a thread.
+
+Method (transparent + explainable):
+1. Demand curve: time-to-departure → price multiplier (U-shape).
+2. Linear regression on the last N price points → slope (per day).
+3. Volatility: stdev / mean of recent prices.
+4. Prediction = base * demand(d) + slope * d_within_window
+5. Confidence interval via volatility band.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import math
+import statistics
+from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
+
+from .database import FlightDB
+
+
+# -------- Demand curve (U-shape: book too early or too late = expensive) -------
+
+def demand_multiplier(days_to_departure: int) -> float:
+    """Return multiplier in roughly [0.85, 1.45].
+    U-shape: cheapest ~21-45 days out, expensive last-minute or way-early.
+    """
+    d = max(0, days_to_departure)
+    if d <= 3:
+        return 1.45
+    if d <= 7:
+        return 1.25
+    if d <= 14:
+        return 1.10
+    if d <= 45:
+        return 0.92
+    if d <= 90:
+        return 1.00
+    return 1.08  # very early
+
+
+# -------- Linear regression (least squares) -------
+
+def linear_fit(points: List[Tuple[float, float]]) -> Tuple[float, float]:
+    """Return (slope, intercept) for y = slope*x + intercept."""
+    n = len(points)
+    if n < 2:
+        return 0.0, points[0][1] if points else 0.0
+    sx = sum(x for x, _ in points)
+    sy = sum(y for _, y in points)
+    sxx = sum(x * x for x, _ in points)
+    sxy = sum(x * y for x, _ in points)
+    denom = n * sxx - sx * sx
+    if denom == 0:
+        return 0.0, sy / n
+    slope = (n * sxy - sx * sy) / denom
+    intercept = (sy - slope * sx) / n
+    return slope, intercept
+
+
+# -------- Predictor -------
+
+@dataclass
+class Prediction:
+    flight_id: int
+    flight_no: str
+    current_price: float
+    predicted_7d: float
+    predicted_14d: float
+    predicted_30d: float
+    trend: str          # "rising" | "falling" | "stable"
+    slope_per_day: float

+"""
+Flight Monitor — real-time price monitor with watchdog + alerts.
+
+Architecture (per architect_feedback §2):
+- `asyncio` for the polling loop
+- retry/backoff on transient failures
+- thread-safe DB writes via the FlightDB wrapper (sqlite3_thread_safe pattern)
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import time
+import asyncio
+import logging
+import random
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List, Callable
+
+from .database import FlightDB
+from .scraper import FlightScraper
+
+logger = logging.getLogger("flight.monitor")
+
+
+class FlightMonitor:
+    """Continuous price poller + alert dispatcher.
+
+    Two modes:
+    - `monitor_prices` loop: periodically append a new price observation
+      to every tracked flight (using the simulator's random walk so the
+      chart visibly changes between polls).
+    - `check_alerts` loop: re-evaluate active price alerts.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, db: FlightDB, scraper: FlightScraper,
+                 poll_interval_sec: float = 30.0,
+                 alert_interval_sec: float = 15.0):
+        self.db = db
+        self.scraper = scraper
+        self.poll_interval = poll_interval_sec
+        self.alert_interval = alert_interval_sec
+        self._running = False
+        self._callbacks: List[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]] = []
+        self.last_event: Dict[str, Any] = {
+            "type": "idle",
+            "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
+        }
+
+    def subscribe(self, cb: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]):
+        self._callbacks.append(cb)
+
+    def _emit(self, event: Dict[str, Any]):
+        self.last_event = event
+        for cb in self._callbacks:
+            try:
+                cb(event)
+            except Exception:
+                logger.exception("monitor subscriber error")
+
+    # -------- Main loop --------
+
+    async def run(self):
+        self._running = True
+        self._emit({"type": "started", "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()})
+        try:
+            while self._running:
+                # Price polling
+                try:
+                    n = self._poll_all_prices()
+                    self._emit({
+                        "type": "price_update",
+                        "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
+                        "points_added": n,
+                    })
+                except Exception as e:
+                    logger.exception("price poll iteration failed")
+                    self._emit({"type": "price_update_error",
+                                "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),

+"""
+Flight Monitor — FastAPI server.
+
+Reuses micro-skills:
+- fastapi_restful_api_development (endpoints + FileResponse)
+- port_allocation_fallback (try preferred port, fall back to 0)
+- sqlite3_thread_safe (FlightDB already wraps it)
+- json_index_export (the dashboard endpoint streams JSON)
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import socket
+import logging
+from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Optional, List
+
+from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, BackgroundTasks
+from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
+from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
+from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
+
+from .database import FlightDB
+from .scraper import FlightScraper
+from .predictor import PricePredictor
+from .monitor import FlightMonitor
+
+# --- Paths & config ---
+
+PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+WEB_OUTPUT = PROJECT_ROOT / "web_output.html"
+DB_PATH = os.environ.get("FLIGHT_DB_PATH",
+                         str(PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "flights.db"))
+LOG_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "logs"
+LOG_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+
+logging.basicConfig(
+    level=logging.INFO,
+    format="%(asctime)s %(name)s %(levelname)s %(message)s",
+    handlers=[logging.FileHandler(LOG_DIR / "server.log"),
+              logging.StreamHandler()],
+)
+logger = logging.getLogger("flight.api")
+
+
+# --- Pydantic schemas ---
+
+class RouteRequest(BaseModel):
+    origin: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=4)
+    destination: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=4)
+    days: int = Field(7, ge=1, le=30)
+
+
+class AlertRequest(BaseModel):
+    flight_id: int
+    threshold_price: float = Field(..., gt=0)
+    direction: str = Field("below", pattern="^(below|above)$")
+
+
+# --- App factory ---
+
+@asynccontextmanager
+async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
+    """Startup / shutdown. Reuses sqlite3_thread_safe pattern."""
+    logger.info("Starting Flight Monitor API — db=%s", DB_PATH)
+    db = FlightDB(DB_PATH)
+    scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode=os.environ.get("FLIGHT_MODE", "simulate"))
+    predictor = PricePredictor(db)
+    monitor = FlightMonitor(
+        db, scraper,
+        poll_interval_sec=float(os.environ.get("FLIGHT_POLL_INTERVAL", "30")),
+    )
+
+    app.state.db = db
+    app.state.scraper = scraper
+    app.state.predictor = predictor
+    app.state.monitor = monitor

+<!DOCTYPE html>
+<html lang="zh-Hant">
+<head>
+<meta charset="UTF-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
+<title>Flight Monitor — 即時航班價格預測儀表板</title>
+<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.0/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
+<style>
+  :root {
+    --bg-0: #0a0e1a;
+    --bg-1: #111827;
+    --bg-2: #1f2937;
+    --bg-3: #374151;
+    --text-0: #f9fafb;
+    --text-1: #d1d5db;
+    --text-2: #9ca3af;
+    --accent: #38bdf8;
+    --accent-2: #818cf8;
+    --green: #34d399;
+    --red: #f87171;
+    --yellow: #fbbf24;
+    --border: #1f2937;
+    --shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.4);
+  }
+  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
+  body {
+    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", "Microsoft JhengHei",
+                 Roboto, sans-serif;
+    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-0) 0%, var(--bg-1) 100%);
+    color: var(--text-0);
+    min-height: 100vh;
+    line-height: 1.5;
+  }
+  header {
+    padding: 24px 32px;
+    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
+    background: rgba(17,24,39,.8);
+    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
+    position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 100;
+  }
+  header h1 {
+    font-size: 24px;
+    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2));
+    -webkit-background-clip: text;
+    background-clip: text;
+    color: transparent;
+  }
+  header .sub { color: var(--text-2); font-size: 13px; margin-top: 4px; }
+  .container {
+    max-width: 1400px;
+    margin: 0 auto;
+    padding: 24px;
+  }
+  .stats-grid {
+    display: grid;
+    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
+    gap: 16px;
+    margin-bottom: 24px;
+  }
+  .stat-card {
+    background: var(--bg-1);
+    border: 1px solid var(--border);
+    border-radius: 12px;
+    padding: 18px;
+    transition: transform .2s, border-color .2s;
+  }
+  .stat-card:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); border-color: var(--accent); }
+  .stat-card .label { color: var(--text-2); font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .05em; }
+  .stat-card .value { font-size: 28px; font-weight: 700; margin-top: 6px; }
+  .stat-card .value.accent { color: var(--accent); }
+  .stat-card .value.green { color: var(--green); }
+  .stat-card .value.yellow { color: var(--yellow); }
+
+  .grid-2 {
+    display: grid;
+    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
+    gap: 24px;
+  }

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
from src.database import FlightDB
from src.scraper import FlightScraper
from src.predictor import PricePredictor

# 1) DB init + scrape a small route
db = FlightDB('data/flights.db')
print('1) DB initialized. Stats:', db.stats())

scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode='simulate')
result = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'NRT', days=3)
print('2) Scrape result:', result)
print('3) Post-scrape stats:', db.stats())

# 4) Get flights list
flights = db.list_flights()
print(f'4) Flights in DB: {len(flights)}')
print(f'   First flight: {flights[0]}')

# 5) Get price history for first flight
fid = flights[0]['id']
history = db.get_price_history(fid)
print(f'5) Price history for flight {fid}: {len(history)} points')
if history:
    print(f'   First: {history[0]}')
    print(f'   Last:  {history[-1]}')

# 6) Predict
predictor = PricePredictor(db)
pred = predictor.predict(fid)
print('6) Prediction:', pred.to_dict() if pred else 'None')

# 7) Predict all
all_preds = predictor.predict_all(limit=5)
print(f'7) Predict-all returned {len(all_preds)} items')
if all_preds:
    print(f'   First item: {all_preds[0]}')

# 8) Add an alert
aid = db.add_alert(fid, threshold_price=50000, direction='below')
print(f'8) Alert created: id={aid}')
alerts = db.active_alerts()
print(f'   Active alerts: {len(alerts)}')

db.close()
print()
print('=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===')
" 2>&1  0.3s [exit 1]

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
from src.database import FlightDB
from src.scraper import FlightScraper
from src.predictor import PricePredictor

# 1) DB init
db = FlightDB('data/flights.db')
print('1) DB initialized. Stats:', db.stats())

# 2) Scrape a small route
scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode='simulate')
result = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'NRT', date_range_days=3)
print('2) Scrape result:', {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})
print('3) Post-scrape stats:', db.stats())

# 4) Flights list
flights = db.list_flights()
print(f'4) Flights in DB: {len(flights)}')

# 5) Price history
fid = flights[0]['id']
history = db.get_price_history(fid)
print(f'5) Price history for flight {fid}: {len(history)} points')
if history:
    print(f'   First: {history[0]}')
    print(f'   Last:  {history[-1]}')

# 6) Predict
predictor = PricePredictor(db)
pred = predictor.predict(fid)
print('6) Prediction:', pred.to_dict() if pred else 'None')

# 7) Predict-all
all_preds = predictor.predict_all(limit=5)
print(f'7) Predict-all returned {len(all_preds)} items')
if all_preds:
    print(f'   Sample: flight_no={all_preds[0][\"flight_no\"]} trend={all_preds[0][\"trend\"]} current={all_preds[0][\"current_price\"]} 7d={all_preds[0][\"predicted_7d\"]} conf={all_preds[0][\"confidence\"]}')

# 8) Alert
aid = db.add_alert(fid, threshold_price=50000, direction='below')
print(f'8) Alert created id={aid}')
alerts = db.active_alerts()
print(f'   Active alerts: {len(alerts)}')

db.close()
print()
print('=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===')

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
from src.database import FlightDB
from src.scraper import FlightScraper
from src.predictor import PricePredictor

db = FlightDB('data/flights.db')
scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode='simulate')
result = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'NRT', date_range_days=3)
print('Scrape:', {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})
print('Stats:', db.stats())

flights = db.list_flights()
print(f'Flights: {len(flights)}')

fid = flights[0]['id']
history = db.get_price_history(fid)
print(f'History for flight {fid}: {len(history)} points, range {history[0][\"price\"]:.0f} → {history[-1][\"price\"]:.0f}')

predictor = PricePredictor(db)
pred = predictor.predict(fid)
print('Prediction:')
for k, v in pred.to_dict().items():
    print(f'  {k}: {v}')

all_preds = predictor.predict_all(limit=5)
print(f'\\nPredict-all: {len(all_preds)} items')
for p in all_preds[:3]:
    print(f'  {p[\"flight_no\"]:>10} {p[\"airline\"]:>3} {p[\"origin\"]}→{p[\"destination\"]} {p[\"departure_date\"]} | now={p[\"current_price\"]:.0f} 7d={p[\"predicted_7d\"]:.0f} trend={p[\"trend\"]} conf={p[\"confidence\"]}')

aid = db.add_alert(fid, threshold_price=50000, direction='below')
print(f'\\nAlert created: id={aid}')
alerts = db.active_alerts()
print(f'Active alerts: {len(alerts)}')

db.close()
print()
print('=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===')

    Now re-run smoke test, then start the server:
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
  ┊ 💻 preparing terminal…
  ┊ 💻 $         rm -f data/flights.db && python3 -c "
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
from src.database import FlightDB
from src.scraper import FlightScraper
from src.predictor import PricePredictor
from src.monitor import FlightMonitor

db = FlightDB('data/flights.db')
scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode='simulate')
monitor = FlightMonitor(db, scraper, poll_interval_sec=2)

# Scrape 2 routes
r1 = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'NRT', date_range_days=5)
print('TPE→NRT:', {k: v for k, v in r1.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})
r2 = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'HKG', date_range_days=5)
print('TPE→HKG:', {k: v for k, v in r2.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})

# Add an alert
flights = db.list_flights()
fid = flights[0]['id']
aid = db.add_alert(fid, threshold_price=20000, direction='below')
print(f'Alert id={aid}')

# Run a single price poll (simulate what the monitor loop does)
n_added = monitor._poll_all_prices()
print(f'Price poll added: {n_added} points')

# Check alerts
triggered = monitor._check_alerts()
print(f'Alerts triggered: {len(triggered)}')

print('Final stats:', db.stats())
db.close()
print()
print('=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===')
" 2>&1  2.0s

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '.')
from src.database import FlightDB
from src.scraper import FlightScraper
from src.predictor import PricePredictor
from src.monitor import FlightMonitor

db = FlightDB('data/flights.db')
scraper = FlightScraper(db, mode='simulate')
monitor = FlightMonitor(db, scraper, poll_interval_sec=2)

r1 = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'NRT', date_range_days=5)
print('TPE→NRT:', {k: v for k, v in r1.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})
r2 = scraper.fetch_route('TPE', 'HKG', date_range_days=5)
print('TPE→HKG:', {k: v for k, v in r2.items() if k != 'scrape_log_id'})

flights = db.list_flights()
fid = flights[0]['id']
aid = db.add_alert(fid, threshold_price=20000, direction='below')
print(f'Alert id={aid}')

n_added = monitor._poll_all_prices()
print(f'Price poll added: {n_added} points')

triggered = monitor._check_alerts()
print(f'Alerts triggered: {len(triggered)}')

print('Final stats:', db.stats())
db.close()
print()
print('=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===')
